% 8.2.20
% For adhy. 8.1 and 8.2, S subst. the foll. passage:
08*0007_01 zibirAd dhastinapuraM prApya bhArata saMjayaH
08*0007_02 praviveza mahAprAjJo dhRtarASTranivezanam
08*0007_03 zokenopahataH sUto vihvalo bhRzaduHkhitaH
08*0007_04 cintayan nidhanaM ghoraM sUtaputrasya pANDavaiH
08*0007_05 antaHpuraM pravizyaiva saMjayo rAjasattama
08*0007_06 azrukaNTho bhRzaM trasto rAjAnam upajagmivAn
08*0007_07 upasthAya ca rAjAnaM viniHzvasya ca sUtajaH
08*0007_08 nAtihRSTamanA rAjann idaM vacanam abravIt
08*0007=08 saMjayaH
08*0007_09 saMjayo 'haM mahArAja namas te bharatarSabha
08*0007_10 hato vaikartanaH karNaH kRtvA karma suduSkaram
08*0007_11 cedikAzikarUzAnAM matsyAnAM somakaiH saha
08*0007_12 kRtvAsau kadanaM zete vAtanunna iva drumaH
08*0007_13 goSThamadhye RSabha iva govrajaiH parivAritaH
08*0007_14 vyAlena nihato yadvat tathAsau nihataH paraiH
08*0007_15 nirAzAn pANDavAn kRtvA jaye rAjan sasAtyakAn
08*0007_16 pAJcAlAnAM rathAMz caiva vinihatya sahasrazaH
08*0007_17 hatvA zUrAn maheSvAsAn vidrAvya ca dizo daza
08*0007_18 hato vaikartanaH karNaH pANDavena kirITinA
08*0007_19 vairasya gatam AnRNyaM durgamasya durAtmabhiH
08*0007_20 hatvA karNaM mahArAja vizalyaH pANDavo 'bhavat
08*0007_21 zoSaNaM sAgarANAM vA pAtanaM zazisUryayoH
08*0007_22 pRthivyA dAraNaM yAdRk tAdRk karNasya pAtanam
08*0007_23 yodhAz ca bahavo rAjan hatAs tatra jayaiSiNaH
08*0007_24 rAjAno rAjaputrAz ca zUrAH parighabAhavaH
08*0007_25 rathaughAz ca naraughAz ca hatA rAjan sahasrazaH
08*0007_26 vAraNA nihatAs tatra vAjinaz ca mahAhave
08*0007_27 kSatriyAz ca mahArAja senayor ubhayor hatAH
08*0007_28 parasparam avekSyAtra parasparakRtAgasaH
08*0007_29 kiMciccheSAn parAn kRtvA tIrtvA pANDavavAhinIm
08*0007_30 pArthavelAM samAsAdya hato vaikartano vRSA
08*0007_31 jayAzA dhArtarASTrANAM vairasya ca mukhaM nRpa
08*0007_32 tIrNaM tat pANDavai rAjan yat purA nAvabudhyase
08*0007_33 procyamAnaM mahArAja bandhubhir hitabuddhibhiH
08*0007_34 tad idaM samanuprAptaM vyasanaM tvAM mahAbhayam
08*0007_35 putrANAM rAjyakAmena tvayA rAjan hitaiSiNA
08*0007_36 caritAny ahitAny eva teSAM te phalam Agatam
08*0007_37 hato duHzAsano rAjan yathoktaM pANDavena tu
08*0007_38 pratijJA bhImasenena nistIrNA sA camUmukhe
08*0007_39 pItaM ca kSatajaM tasya dhArtarASTrasya sayuge
08*0007_40 pANDavena mahArAja kRtvA karma suduSkaram
